Petblocks是由Shynixn制作的插件！它可以将任意方块变成宠物！怎么样，还不心动？
赶快来吧-->捐助
方块宠物WiKi
正在翻译中
原WiKi：https://shynixn.github.io/PetBlocks/build/html/index.html
特点
每个玩家都有宠物
GUI设置，超级简便
自定义宠物皮肤
粒子效果自定义
配置文件高度自定义化
宠物重命名
已汉化
配置文件：
GUI完全可配置
新宠物皮肤增添
自定义宠物ai
支持MySQL
支持跨服宠物
演示视频&图片&材质包
材质包下载：dropBox
演示视频:

https://www.bilibili.com/video/av66263538/

演示图片:


命令&配置
命令:
/petblock - 打开GUI
/petblock toggle - 切换宠物状态
/petblock call - 召唤宠物
/petblock rename 名字 - 宠物重命名
/petblock skin 名字 - 宠物皮肤更改
/petblocks 管理员命令
/petblockreload - 重载配置
权限文件请看：配置：每条命令对应每条权限
配置文件（已汉化）:

配置文件在这呢

  1. #######################################################################
  2. #                          PetBlocks                                  #
  3. #                                        by Shynixn                                                              #
  4. #######################################################################

  6. # You can get started and find the permissions at the official documentation.
  7. # 你可以从这里查询权限/有关此插件的帮助
  8. # https://shynixn.github.io/PetBlocks

  10. ############################

  12. # Data-collecting settings
  13. # 数据收集设置

  15. # These are settings to send anonymous stats to https://bstats.org/plugin/bukkit/PetBlocks.
  16. # As this does not have an impact on your PetBlocks experience at all I would like you to set it on true as knowing that my
  17. # plugin gets used by many server helps me focusing on new updates ;)
  18. # 别紧张，我们启用这个数据收集只是为了调查我的插件到底有多少个服务器使用这个插件，并且我会对这个插件一直负责到底
  19. # 如果你想满足作者的好奇心，就设置为true吧
  20. # 收集的数据的连接：https://bstats.org/plugin/bukkit/PetBlocks.

  22. ############################

  24. metrics: true

  26. ############################

  28. # Config version
  29. # 配置文件版本

  31. # Change the config-version when you have read the update log and see a change of
  32. # the config.yml. The config.yml does not change every update and the plugin automatically
  33. # checks if your config-version is compatible with the installed plugin.jar.
  34. # 当你阅读了更新日志的时候，你可以对照着更新日志更新的配置文件来进行版本号的修改和内容的增添
  35. # config.yml不会自动更新，不过他会检测当前插件是否与该配置文件兼容

  37. ############################

  39. config-version: 1

  41. ############################

  43. # Database settings
  44. # 数据库设置

  46. # All pets are stored in a SQLite database per default which is basically the
  47. # PetBlocks.db file in the plugin folder.
  48. # Available types are 'sqlite' and 'mysql'.
  49. # Use the same mysql database for 2 or more server in order to allow cross server pets.
  50. # 所有宠物都存储在数据库里边（这是平常情况）
  51. # 宠物信息在文件夹的PetBlocks.db中

  53. ############################

  55. sql:
  56.   type: 'sqlite'
  57.   host: localhost
  58.   port: 3306
  59.   database: ''
  60.   usessl: false
  61.   table-prefix: 'SHY'
  62.   username: ''
  63.   password: ''

  65. ############################

  67. # Command settings
  68. # 命令设置

  70. # There are commands available in PetBlocks you can customize by yourself.
  71. # This means you can disable the command entirely, change the name, description
  72. # and even permission.
  73. # 这个命令配置你可以自己更改
  74. # 你可以更改命令，更改描述，甚至能更改权限

  76. ############################

  78. commands:
  79.   petblock:
  80.     # 是否启用命令
  81.     enabled: true
  82.     # 命令
  83.     command: petblock
  84.     useage: /<command>
  85.     # 描述
  86.     description: Opens the pet GUI.
  87.     # 权限
  88.     permission: petblocks.command.use
  89.     # 没有权限时提示的信息
  90.     permission-message: You don't have permission

  92. ############################

  94. # Messages settings
  95. # 信息设置

  97. # Change the messages of the PetBlocks plugin.
  98. # 这里能更改所有信息

  100. ############################

  102. messages:
  103.   # 插件前缀
  104.   prefix: '&2[&aPetBlocks&2]&a '
  105.   # GUI页面的标题
  106.   gui-title: 'PetBlocks'
  107.   # 重命名的前缀
  108.   rename-suggest-prefix: ''
  109.   # 重命名的描述（lore）
  110.   rename-suggest-clickable: '&e<<单击这里>> '
  111.   # 重命名的后缀
  112.   rename-suggest-suffix: '&a更改你的宠物的名字.'
  113.   # 重命名的确认
  114.   rename-suggest-hover: '点我更改你宠物的名字.'
  115.   # 成功更改了
  116.   rename-success: '&a你成功更改了你宠物的名字.'
  117.   # 重命名错误
  118.   rename-error: '&c你似乎不能那样子做喔.'
  119.   # 自定义皮肤的前缀
  120.   customhead-suggest-prefix: ''
  121.   # 自定义皮肤的描述
  122.   customhead-suggest-clickable: '&e<<单击这里>> '
  123.   # 自定义皮肤的后缀
  124.   customhead-suggest-suffix: '&a更改你宠物的皮肤.'
  125.   # 自定义皮肤的确认
  126.   customhead-suggest-hover: '点我更改你宠物的皮肤.'
  127.   # 自定义皮肤成功
  128.   customhead-success: '&a你成功更改了你宠物的皮肤.'
  129.   # 自定义皮肤错误
  130.   customhead-error: '&e你似乎不能那样子做喔.'
  131.   # 召唤成功
  132.   called-success: '&a你把宠物召唤过来了.'
  133.   # 召唤发生错误
  134.   called-error: '&c宠物现在不知干嘛了，召唤不过来耶.'
  135.   # 宠物无法重生
  136.   despawn: '&a你的宠物无法重生了.'
  137.   # 拥有权限为true
  138.   has-permission: '&a有'
  139.   # 拥有权限为false
  140.   has-no-permission: '&c没有'
  141.   # 你没有权限的提示
  142.   no-permission: '&c你没有权限.'

  144. ############################

  146. # Global settings
  147. # 全局设置

  149. # These are global settings which belong to the whole PetBlocks plugin without being specific to a single pet.
  150. # Apply-Pet-On-First-spawn requires pet.enabled be set true.
  151. # 这里是全局设置而不是针对单个宠物
  152. # Apply-Pet-On-First-spawn必须要设置为true

  154. # Please take a look into the official documentation mentioned at the top of this file if you need an explanation for all values.
  155. # 如果你要查看这些说明，请打开官方WiKi

  157. ############################

  159. global-configuration:
  160.   respawn-delay: 4
  161.   teleport-delay: 0
  162.   teleport-fall: true
  163.   fly-wall-colision: true
  164.   apply-pet-on-first-spawn: false
  165.   overwrite-previous-pet: false
  166.   sounds-other-players: true
  167.   particles-other-players: true
  168.   disable-on-sneak:
  169.     - 'wearing'
  170.     - 'ground-riding'
  171.     - 'fly-riding'
  172.   max-petname-length: 20
  173.   petname-blacklist:
  174.     - 'petty'
  175.     - 'shit'

  177. ############################

  179. # Default Pet settings
  180. # 默认宠物设置

  182. # The pets of this plugin are very, very customizeable.
  183. # Please take a look into the official documentation mentioned at the top of this file
  184. # in order to configure your pet correctly.
  185. # 本插件的宠物的自定义性非常的高！
  186. # 为了正确的配置宠物，请查阅官方WiKi

  188. # These are the settings players are starting with when they get their first pet. However,
  189. # you can add all of these settings to the GUI in order to change them per skin or particle or something else.
  190. # 这是玩家获得的第一只宠物的设置
  191. # 你可以将这些设置添加进GUI设置里面，以便玩家配置他们的宠物

  193. # You can find a list of all available ais on the documentation.
  194. # 你可以查阅WiKi来配置该选项

  196. ############################

  198. pet:
  199.   enabled: false
  200.   name: "<player>的宠物"
  201.   sound-enabled: true
  202.   particle-enabled: true
  203.   skin:
  204.     id: 2
  205.   add-ai:
  206.     1:
  207.       type: 'hopping'
  208.       climbing-height: 1.0
  209.       speed: 1.0
  210.       offset-y: -1.0

  219.         offx: 0

  247.         name: 'CHICKEN_IDLE'
  248.         volume: 5.0
  249.         pitch: 1.0
  250. ############################

  252. # World settings
  253. # 世界设置

  255. # These settings allow spawning or deny spawning pets in certain worlds.
  256. # 这里可以设置可以宠物重生或不能重生的世界

  258. # world-excluded: Add worlds where the pets should not be able to spawn.
  259. # world-excluded：添加到这里的世界，宠物将不能在这个世界重生
  260. # (When 'all' is added to excluded all worlds are disabled for spawning except the added worlds to included)
  261. # (当设置为all时，所有世界都不能重生你的宠物)
  262. # world-included: Add worlds where the pets should be able to spawn.
  263. # world-excluded：添加到这里的世界，宠物将能在这个世界重生
  264. # (When 'all' is added to included all worlds are enabled for spawning except the added worlds to excluded)
  265. # (当设置为all时，所有世界都能重生你的宠物)

  267. # DO NOT PUT 'all' into both included and excluded!
  268. # 千万不要两个都写all，不然会出现意想不到的BUG，可能数据清空，更可能..我也不好说

  270. ############################

  272. world:
  273.   excluded:
  274.     - ''
  275.   included:
  276.     - 'all'

  278. ############################

  280. # Static GUI settings.
  281. # 静态GUI设置

  283. # These settings are global settings for the gui which do not get dynamically parsed.
  284. # 这的设置是GUI的全局设置，无法变成实时更新的

  286. # Adding new items is not possible for this settings and you can only change the parameters of the existing declared ones.
  287. # 该设置无法添加新的格子，只能更改已有的参数.

  289. ############################

  291. static-gui:
  292.   empty-slot:
  293.     icon:
